KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending
			KOR_review_valid_df_foreign_purchase_board = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board }
			USA = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA
		}
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_df_foreign_purchase_board
		}
		USA = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_df_foreign_purchase_board
		}
		KOR = {
			# Agreement registration.
		}
		USA = {
			# Agreement registration.
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.0012 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			USA = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
		USA = { send_equipment = { type = mdcw_infantry_weapons_1955 amount = 600 target = KOR } }
	}
}
KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_send = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			KOR_review_valid_df_foreign_purchase_board = yes
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_df_foreign_purchase_board
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_cooldown }
		}
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending days = 30 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_cooldown days = 90 }
	}
}
KOR_review_offer_df_licensed_armaments_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_licensed_armaments_pending
			KOR_review_valid_df_licensed_armaments = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_licensed_armaments }
			USA = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_licensed_armaments_assent_USA
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_licensed_armaments_pending
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_licensed_armaments_assent_USA
		}
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_licensed_armaments
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_df_licensed_armaments
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_programme_df_licensed_armaments }
		}
		USA = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_licensed_armaments
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_df_licensed_armaments
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_df_licensed_armaments_USA }
		}
		KOR = {
			# Agreement registration.
		}
		USA = {
			# Agreement registration.
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.001 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			USA = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

KOR_review_offer_japan_compensation_fund_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_japan_compensation_fund_pending
			KOR_review_valid_japan_compensation_fund = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_japan_compensation_fund }
			JAP = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_japan_compensation_fund_assent_JAP
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_japan_compensation_fund_pending
		JAP = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_japan_compensation_fund_assent_JAP
		}
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_japan_compensation_fund
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_japan_compensation_fund
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_programme_japan_compensation_fund }
		}
		JAP = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_japan_compensation_fund
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_japan_compensation_fund
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_japan_compensation_fund_JAP }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0036 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		JAP = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0016 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = JAP.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.0045 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			JAP = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_retain_taipei_pending
		TAI = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_retain_taipei_assent_TAI
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_offer_retain_taipei_duration days = 1825 }
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_retain_taipei
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_retain_taipei
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_programme_retain_taipei }
		}
		TAI = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_retain_taipei
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_retain_taipei
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_retain_taipei_TAI }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.002 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = TAI modifier = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI }
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI_live
			set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI_term days = 1825 }
		}
		TAI = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0014 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = KOR modifier = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_TAI_KOR }
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_TAI_KOR_live
			set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_TAI_KOR_term days = 1825 }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_review_recognition_set
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { recognised_countries = TAI } }
			}
			add_to_array = { recognised_countries = TAI }
		}
		TAI = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_in_array = { recognised_countries = KOR } }
				}
				add_to_array = { recognised_countries = KOR }
			}
		}
	}

			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_moscow_normalization_pending
		SOV = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_moscow_normalization_assent_SOV
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_offer_moscow_normalization_duration days = 1825 }
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_moscow_normalization
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_moscow_normalization
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_programme_moscow_normalization }
		}
		SOV = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_moscow_normalization
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_moscow_normalization
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_moscow_normalization_SOV }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0032 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_KOR_SOV }
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_KOR_SOV_live
			set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_KOR_SOV_term days = 1825 }
		}
		SOV = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0024 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = KOR modifier = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_SOV_KOR }
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_SOV_KOR_live
			set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_opinion_moscow_normalization_SOV_KOR_term days = 1825 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { recognised_countries = SOV } }
			}
			add_to_array = { recognised_countries = SOV }
		}
		SOV = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_in_array = { recognised_countries = KOR } }
				}
				add_to_array = { recognised_countries = KOR }
			}
		}
	}
}

			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_moscow_credit_protocol

			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_moscow_credit_protocol

		SOV = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0012 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.006 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			set_variable = { KOR_review_principal = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			SOV = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_interkorean_manufacturing_pending
			KOR_review_valid_interkorean_manufacturing = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_interkorean_manufacturing }
			NKO = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_interkorean_manufacturing_assent_NKO
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_interkorean_manufacturing_pending
		NKO = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_interkorean_manufacturing_assent_NKO
		}
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_interkorean_manufacturing
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_interkorean_manufacturing
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_programme_interkorean_manufacturing }
		}
		NKO = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_interkorean_manufacturing
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_interkorean_manufacturing
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_interkorean_manufacturing_NKO }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0045 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = COLDWAR_KOR_interkorean_manufacturing
				bonus = 0.35
				uses = 1
				ahead_reduction = 0
				category = CAT_production_lines
			}
		}
		NKO = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.002 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.0015 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }

			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_kedo_funding_NKO }
		}
		USA = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_kedo_funding
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_kedo_funding
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_kedo_funding_USA }
		}
		JAP = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_kedo_funding
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_kedo_funding
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_kedo_funding_JAP }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0032 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		NKO = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0012 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.001 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.001 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.003 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			NKO = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_peninsula_health_aid_pending
			KOR_review_valid_peninsula_health_aid = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_peninsula_health_aid }
			NKO = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_peninsula_health_aid_assent_NKO
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_peninsula_health_aid_pending
		NKO = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_peninsula_health_aid_assent_NKO
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_offer_peninsula_health_aid_duration days = 365 }
		KOR = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_peninsula_health_aid
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_peninsula_health_aid
		}
		NKO = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_peninsula_health_aid
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_peninsula_health_aid
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_partner_peninsula_health_aid_NKO }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0035 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		NKO = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0008 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = KOR.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled = 0.0025 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_scheduled > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_transfer_amount = KOR_review_scheduled }
			KOR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			NKO = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = KOR.KOR_review_transfer_amount }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_accession_to_north
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_accession_to_north
		}
		NKO = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_accession_to_north
			set_country_flag = KOR_review_live_accession_to_north
		}
		KOR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0034 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		NKO = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0025 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_review_union_treaty_set
		set_country_flag = KOR_review_union_route_accession_to_north
	}
}

KOR_review_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { COLDWAR_KOR_campaign = yes has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect }
		}
		KOR_review_migrate = yes
		KOR_review_sync_programmes = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = KOR
			}
			KOR_review_sync_offers = yes
			KOR_review_sync_korean_diplomacy = yes
			KOR = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI_live
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI_term }
					}
					remove_opinion_modifier = { target = TAI modifier = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI }
					clr_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_KOR_TAI_live
				}
			}
			TAI = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_review_opinion_retain_taipei_TAI_KOR_live

KOR_review_un_start = {
	set_global_flag = { flag = KOR_review_UN_pending days = 60 }
	clr_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_veto
	set_variable = { global.KOR_review_UN_support = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = global.security_council_members clr_country_flag = KOR_review_UN_voted }
}
KOR_review_un_tally = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_pending
			has_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_veto
		}
		clr_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_pending
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_pending
			check_variable = { var = global.KOR_review_UN_support value = 9 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = united_nations_member }
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { global.UN_general_assembly = THIS }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = united_nations_member }
		clr_global_flag = KOR_review_UN_pending
	}
}
KOR_review_snapshot_regions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = NKO
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_south_gdpc = gdp_per_capita }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_north_gdpc = NKO.gdp_per_capita }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_south_pop = population_total_m }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_north_pop = NKO.population_total_m }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_kor_infra = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_kor_infra_population = 0 }
		604 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		605 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		606 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		607 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_kor_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_kor_infra_population > 0 }
			}
			divide_variable = { KOR_review_kor_infra = KOR_review_kor_infra_population }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_nko_infra = 0 }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_nko_infra_population = 0 }
		601 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		602 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		603 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		1199 = {
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra = { value = building_level@infrastructure multiply = state_population_k } }
			add_to_variable = { KOR.KOR_review_nko_infra_population = state_population_k }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_nko_infra_population > 0 }
			}
			divide_variable = { KOR_review_nko_infra = KOR_review_nko_infra_population }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_regional_gap = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { check_variable = { KOR_review_south_gdpc > 0 } check_variable = { KOR_review_north_gdpc > 0 } }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_larger_gdpc = { value = KOR_review_south_gdpc max = KOR_review_north_gdpc } }
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_smaller_gdpc = { value = KOR_review_south_gdpc min = KOR_review_north_gdpc } }
			set_variable = { KOR_review_regional_gap = { value = 1 subtract = { value = KOR_review_smaller_gdpc divide = KOR_review_larger_gdpc } } }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_population_share = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { check_variable = { KOR_review_south_pop > 0 } check_variable = { KOR_review_north_pop > 0 } }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_total_pop = { value = KOR_review_south_pop add = KOR_review_north_pop } }
			set_variable = { KOR_review_population_share = { value = KOR_review_south_pop min = KOR_review_north_pop multiply = 2 divide = KOR_review_total_pop } }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_infrastructure_gap = { value = KOR_review_kor_infra subtract = KOR_review_nko_infra } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_review_infrastructure_gap < 0 }
			}
			multiply_variable = { KOR_review_infrastructure_gap = -1 }
		}
		divide_variable = { KOR_review_infrastructure_gap = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_verification_commission
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_joint_factory_audit
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_freight_timetable
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_grid_reserve_programme
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_civilian_exchange_board
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { KOR_review_prepared = 0.08 }
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_initial_burden = { value = KOR_review_regional_gap multiply = 0.3 add = { value = KOR_review_population_share multiply = 0.25 } add = { value = KOR_review_infrastructure_gap multiply = 0.25 } add = 0.2 multiply = KOR_review_prepared } }
		clamp_variable = { var = KOR_review_initial_burden min = 0 max = 1 }
	}
}
KOR_review_start_union = {
	set_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect
	clr_country_flag = KOR_review_final_union_consent
	set_variable = { KOR_review_union_administration = KOR_review_initial_burden }
	set_variable = { KOR_review_union_economy = KOR_review_initial_burden }
	set_variable = { KOR_review_union_social = KOR_review_initial_burden }
	set_variable = { KOR_review_union_defence = KOR_review_initial_burden }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_review_union_integration }
	KOR_review_sync_union = yes
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
}
KOR_review_sync_union = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect
		}
		set_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_administration = KOR_review_union_administration }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_administration = 0.12 }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_economy = KOR_review_union_economy }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_economy = -0.08 }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_social = KOR_review_union_social }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_social = -0.06 }
		set_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_defence = KOR_review_union_defence }
		multiply_variable = { KOR_review_union_display_defence = -0.06 }
	}
}
KOR_review_union_week = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_tick_done }
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = KOR_review_union_tick_done days = 7 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_administration
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_local_jurisdiction
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_public_accounts
				check_variable = { KOR_review_union_administration > 0 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_review_union_administration = 0.005 }
			clamp_variable = { var = KOR_review_union_administration min = 0 max = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_development_audit
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_rail_rehabilitation
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_grid_repair
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_supplier_transition
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_regional_finance
				check_variable = { KOR_review_union_economy > 0 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_review_union_economy = 0.005 }
			clamp_variable = { var = KOR_review_union_economy min = 0 max = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_social_registry
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_health_access
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_pension_credits
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_employment_services
				check_variable = { KOR_review_union_social > 0 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_review_union_social = 0.005 }
			clamp_variable = { var = KOR_review_union_social min = 0 max = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_armed_forces_register
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_joint_command_reform
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_service_transition
				check_variable = { KOR_review_union_defence > 0 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_review_union_defence = 0.005 }
			clamp_variable = { var = KOR_review_union_defence min = 0 max = 1 }
		}
		KOR_review_sync_union = yes
	}
}
KOR_review_launch_postwar_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_variable = KOR_review_initial_burden }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_initial_burden = 0.2 }
		}
		601 = {
			add_core_of = KOR
		}
		602 = {
			add_core_of = KOR
		}
		603 = {
			add_core_of = KOR
		}
		1199 = {
			add_core_of = KOR
		}
		KOR_review_start_union = yes
	}
}
KOR_review_ratify_union = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = KOR
			country_exists = NKO
			KOR = { NOT = { has_war_with = NKO } }
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_treaty_set
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_final_union_consent
			NKO = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_final_union_consent
			}
		}
		KOR_review_snapshot_regions = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_route_accession_to_north
			}
			KOR_review_suspend_external_on_union = yes
			NKO = {
				set_variable = { KOR_review_initial_burden = KOR.KOR_review_initial_burden }
				604 = {
					add_core_of = NKO
				}
				605 = {
					add_core_of = NKO
				}
				606 = {
					add_core_of = NKO
				}
				607 = {
					add_core_of = NKO
				}
				KOR_review_start_union = yes
				set_country_flag = KOR_review_enacted_union_ratification
				complete_national_focus = COLDWAR_KOR_union_ratification
				add_to_variable = { treasury = KOR.treasury }
				add_to_variable = { debt = KOR.debt }
				add_to_variable = { int_investments = KOR.int_investments }
				annex_country = { target = KOR transfer_troops = yes }
			}
		}
		else = {
			601 = {
				add_core_of = KOR
			}
			602 = {
				add_core_of = KOR
			}
			603 = {
				add_core_of = KOR
			}
			1199 = {
				add_core_of = KOR
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_route_new_common_constitution
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_review_common_constitution
			}
			add_to_variable = { treasury = NKO.treasury }
			add_to_variable = { debt = NKO.debt }
			add_to_variable = { int_investments = NKO.int_investments }
			annex_country = { target = NKO transfer_troops = yes }
			KOR_review_start_union = yes
		}
	}
}

KOR_review_sync_korean_diplomacy = {
	KOR_review_un_tally = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cw_oly_host_now
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_review_host_cycle_seen
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_review_host_cycle_seen
			NOT = { has_country_flag = cw_oly_host_now }
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_review_previous_olympic_cycle
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_union_in_effect }
		}
		KOR_review_snapshot_regions = yes
	}
}
KOR_review_apply_grid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = KOR tag = NKO }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = KOR
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_review_pretrade_power = energy_sum }
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_review_pretrade_power = energy_consumption }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KOR
				country_exists = NKO
				KOR = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_border_power_supply
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = KOR
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { energy_sum = 0.2 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { energy_sum = 0.2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KOR
				country_exists = NKO
				KOR = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_review_live_grid_reserve_programme
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = KOR
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { energy_sum = 0.15 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { energy_sum = 0.15 }
			}
		}
	}
}
